The Tenderer shall be deemed to have read and fully understood the requirements\nof the above Act and Regulations and to have allowed for all costs in compliance therewith.\nThe Contractor will not be allowed to start with any construction works until his Health\nand Safety Plan is approved by the Health and Safety Agent.\nTenderers are to note that the Contractor is required to ensure that all Sub-Contractors or\nothers engaged in the performance of the contract also comply with the above requirements.\nThe Contractor shall prepare and maintain a Health and Safety File in respect of the project,\nwhich shall be available for inspection on Site at all times and handed over to the Employer\non Final Completion of the project.\nThe Contractor is required to submit to the Employer the Occupational Health and Safety\nAgreement (included in C 1.4 of the Contract document) and a letter of good standing from\nthe Compensation Commissioner, or a licensed Compensation Insurer, within 14 (fourteen)\ndays after the Commencement Date of the Contract.\nF.4.2 Eligibility with respect to Expanded Public Works Programme\nThis Contract does not qualify for consideration as an Expanded Public Works Program\nproject, but he Contractor shall make use of local labour as far as possible. Where manual\nlabour is required, remuneration must be paid according to the minimum wages for the region\nPart T1: Tendering Data T 1 - 20 T 1.2\nTender Number: NC/15/2026 Tender Notice and Invitation 15/2026\n\nand in accordance with the latest published “Guidelines for the Implementation of Labour-\nIntensive Infrastructure Projects under the Expanded Public Works Programme (Epwp)”.\nMonthly project reporting will be done on all the EPWP reporting documentation attached in\nAnnexure A of Part 3: Scope of Works of this Tender Document.\nF.4.3 Claims arising after submission of tender\nNo claim for any extras arising out of any doubt or obscurity as to the true intent and\nmeaning of anything shown on the Contract Drawings or contained in the Conditions of\nContract, Scope of Work and Pricing Data, will be admitted by the Employer after the\nsubmission of any tender and the Tenderer shall be deemed to have:\n(a) Inspected the Contract Drawings and read and fully understood the Conditions of\nContract;\n(b) Read and fully understood the whole text of the Scope of Work and Pricing Data and\nthoroughly acquainted himself with the nature of the works proposed and generally of\nall matters which may influence the Contract;\n(c) Visited the site of the proposed works, carefully examined existing conditions, the\nmeans of access to the Site, the conditions under which the work is to be done, and\nacquainted himself with the limitations or restrictions that may be imposed by the\nMunicipality or other Authorities in regard to access and transport of materials, plant\nand equipment to and from the site and made the necessary provisions for any\nadditional costs involved thereby.\n(d) Requested the Employer or his duly authorized agent clearly intelligible to the Tenderer.\n(e) Received any Addenda to the tender documents which have been issued in\naccordance with the Employer’s supply Chain Management Policy.\nBefore submission of any tender, the Tenderer should check the number of pages, and\nif any are found to be missing or duplicated, or the figures or writing indistinct, or if the\nPricing Data contain any obvious errors, the Tenderer must apply to the Employer's\nagent at once to have the same rectified, as no liability will be admitted by the Employer\nin respect of errors in any tender due to the foregoing.\nF.4.4 Community Liaison Officer\nIt is a requirement of the Contract that a Community Liaison Officer (CLO) for the project shall\nbe appointed by the Contractor. The primary functions of the CLO shall be to assist the\nContractor with the selection and recruitment of labour, to represent the local community in\nmatters concerning the use of labour on the works, and to assist with and facilitate\ncommunication between the Contractor, the Principal Agent and the local communities. The\nappointment of the CLO will be as by the guidelines/instructions of the Implementing Agent\nand the local Municipality.\nThe remuneration of the CLO to be fixed at R 7500-00 per month as per the current rate for\nCLO’s in the local authority.\nThe method of identifying suitable candidates for the position of CLO will be through\nadvertisement throughout the community and local Municipality, interviews will be held\nwith candidates and the representative of the Department will be present.\nF.4.5 Invalid tenders\nTenders shall be considered invalid and shall be endorsed and recorded as such in the tender\nPart T1: Tendering Data T 1 - 21 T 1.2\nTender Number: NC/15/2026 Tender Notice and Invitation 15/2026\n\nopening record, by the official responsible who opened the tender, in the following\ncircumstances:\n(a) If the tender offer (the tender price/amount) is not submitted on the Form of Offer and\nAcceptance bound into this tender document (Form C 1.1: Part C 1: Agreement and\nContract Data);\n(b) If the tender is not completed in non-erasable black ink;\n(c) If the Form of Offer and Acceptance is signed, the name of the Tenderer is not stated\nor is indecipherable.\nF.4.6 Negotiations with preferred Tenderers\nThe Employer may negotiate the final terms of a contract with Tenderers identified through a\ncompetitive tendering process as preferred Tenderers, provided that such negotiation:\n(a) does not allow any preferred Tenderer a second or unfair opportunity;\n(b) is not to the detriment of any other Tenderer; and\n(c) does not lead to a higher price than the quotation as submitted.\nMinutes of any such negotiations shall be kept for record purposes.\nF.4.7 General Supply Chain Management Conditions applicable to tenders\nIn terms of its Supply Chain Management Policy, the Employer may not consider a tender\nunless the provider who submitted the tender:\na) has furnished the Employer with that provider's:\n• full name;\n• identification number or company or other registration number; and\n• tax reference number and VAT registration number, if any;\n• Certificate of attendance at a compulsory site inspection, where applicable\nb) has indicated whether:\n• the provider is in the service of the state, or has been in the service of the state in\nthe previous twelve months;\n• the provider is not a natural person, whether any of the directors, managers, principal\nshareholders or stakeholders is in the service of the state, or has been in the\nservice of the state in the previous twelve months; or\n• whether a spouse, child or parent of the provider or of a director, manager, share-\nholder or stakeholder referred to above is in the service of the state, or has been\nin the service of the state in the previous twelve months.\nIrrespective of the procurement process followed, the Employer is prohibited from\nmaking an award to:\n• a person who is in the service of the state;\n• a juristic entity of which any director, manager, principal shareholder or stakeholder\nis in the service of the state;\n• an advisor or consultant contracted with the Employer; or\n• a person, advisor or corporate entity involved with the bid specification committee, or\na director of such corporate entity.\nIn this regard, Tenderers shall complete Schedule 2, Part T2.2: Returnable Schedules:\nCompulsory Enterprise Questionnaire. Failure to complete this schedule may result in the\ntender not being considered.\nPart T1: Tendering Data T 1 - 22 T 1.2\nTender Number: NC/15/2026 Tender Notice and Invitation 15/2026\n\nF.4.8 Combating abuse of the Supply Chain Management Policy\nIn terms of the Its Supply Chain Management Policy, the Employer may reject the tender of\nany Tenderer if that Tenderer or any of its Directors has:\n(a) failed, during the last five years, to perform satisfactorily on a previous contract with the\nEmployer or any other organ of state after written notice was given to that Tenderer\nthat performance was unsatisfactory;\n(b) abused the supply chain management system of the Employer or has committed\nany improper conduct in relation to this system;\n(c) been convicted of fraud or corruption during the past five years;\n(d) willfully neglected, reneged on or failed to comply with any government, municipal\nor other public-sector contract during the past five years; or\n(e) been listed with the Register of Tender Defaulters in terms of section 29 of the\nPrevention and Combating of Corrupt Activities Act (No 12 of 2004) or has been\nlisted on National Treasury's database as a person or juristic entity prohibited from\ndoing business with the public sector.\nF.4.9 UIF payments\nThe Tenderer shall submit to the Employer a letter from the Industrial Council indicating his\nor her good standing with regard to UIF payments upon being requested to do so.\nF.4.10 Registration with Bargaining Council\nTenderers must be registered with a relevant Bargaining Council (if such be in place) and\nprovide the applicable Certificate of Compliance in terms of the relevant Government Gazette.\nF.4.11 Price Variations\nThe tendered Sum shall not be subject to contract price adjustment in accordance with\nthe General Conditions of Contract. GENERAL CONDITIONS\n1.1 The following preference point systems are applicable to invitations to tender:\n- the 80/20 system for requirements with a Rand value of up to R50 000 000 (all applicable taxes\nincluded); and\n- the 90/10 system for requirements with a Rand value above R50 000 000 (all applicable taxes\nincluded).\n1.2 To be completed by the organ of state\na) The applicable preference point system for this tender is the 80/20 preference point system.\n1.3 Points for this tender (even in the case of a tender for income-generating contracts) shall be awarded\nfor:\n(a) Price; and\n(b) Specific Goals.\n1.4 To be completed by the organ of state:\nThe maximum points for this tender are allocated as follows:\nPOINTS\nPRICE 80\nSPECIFIC GOALS 20\nTotal points for Price and SPECIFIC GOALS 100\n1.5 Failure on the part of a tenderer to submit proof or documentation required in terms of this tender to\nclaim points for specific goals with the tender, will be interpreted to mean that preference points for\nspecific goals are not claimed.\n1.6 The organ of state reserves the right to require of a tenderer, either before a tender is adjudicated or\nat any time subsequently, to substantiate any claim in regard to preferences, in any manner required\nby the organ of state.\n1. DEFINITIONS\n(a) “tender” means a written offer in the form determined by an organ of state in response to an invitation\nto provide goods or services through price quotations, competitive tendering process or any other\nmethod envisaged in legislation;\n(b) “price” means an amount of money tendered for goods or services, and includes all applicable taxes\nless all unconditional discounts;\n(c) “rand value” means the total estimated value of a contract in Rand, calculated at the time of bid\ninvitation, and includes all applicable taxes;\n(d) “tender for income-generating contracts” means a written offer in the form determined by an organ\nof state in response to an invitation for the origination of income-generating contracts through any\nmethod envisaged in legislation that will result in a legal agreement between the organ of state and a\nthird party that produces revenue for the organ of state, and includes, but is not limited to, leasing and\ndisposal of assets and concession contracts, excluding direct sales and disposal of assets through\npublic auctions; and\n(e) “the Act” means the Preferential Procurement Policy Framework Act, 2000 (Act No. 5 of 2000).\nPart T 2: Returnable Documents T 2 - 16 T 2.1\nTender Number: NC/15/2026 List of Returnable Schedules: 15/2026\n\nSBD 6.1\n1. Bid rigging is, therefore,\nan agreement between competitors not to compete.\nPart T 2: Returnable Documents T 2 - 24 T 2.1\nTender Number: NC/15/2026 List of Returnable Schedules: 15/2026\n\nSBD 9\nCERTIFICATE OF INDEPENDENT BID DETERMINATION\nI, the undersigned, in submitting the accompanying bid:\n---\n(Bid Number and Description)\nin response to the invitation for the bid made by:\n---\n(Name of Institution)\ndo hereby make the following statements that I certify to be true and complete in every respect:\nI certify, on behalf of:_______________________________________________________that:\n(Name of Bidder)\n1. The Chief Executive Officer of the Contractor shall assume the responsibility in terms of Section 16(1)\nof the Occupational Health and Safety Act (as amended). Should the Contractor assign any duty in\nterms of Section 16(2), a copy of such assignment shall immediately be provided to the\nrepresentative of the Employer as defined in the Contract.\n1. All work performed on the Employer's premises shall be performed under the supervision of the\nConstruction Supervisor who understands the hazards associated with any work that the\nContractor performs on the site in terms of Construction Regulations 2014.\n1. The Contractor shall appoint a Competent Person who shall be trained on any occupational health\nand safety aspect pertaining to them or to the work that is to be performed.\n1. The Contractor shall ensure that he familiarizes himself with the requirements of the Occupational\nHealth and Safety Act and that he, his employees, and any Sub-Contractors, comply with them.\n1. Discipline in the interests of occupational health and safety shall be strictly enforced.\n2. Personal protective equipment shall be issued by the Contractor as required and shall be worn at all\ntimes where necessary.\n1. Written safe work procedures and appropriate precautionary measures shall be available and enforced,\nand all employees shall be made conversant with the contents of these practices.\n1. No substandard equipment / machinery / articles or substances shall be used on the site.\n2. All incidents referred to in terms of Section 24 of the Occupational Health and Safety Act shall be\nreported by the Contractor to the Department of Labour and the Employer.\n10 The Employer hereby obtains an interest in the issue of any formal inquiry conducted in terms of\nSection 32 of the Occupational Health and Safety Act and into any incident involving a Contractor\nand /or his employees and/or his Sub-Contractors.\n1. No use shall be made of any of the Employer's machinery / plant/ equipment /substance/ personal\nprotective equipment or any other article without prior arrangement and written approval.\n1. No alcohol or any other intoxicating substance shall be allowed on the site. Any person suspected of\nbeing under the influence of alcohol or any other intoxicating substance shall not be permitted access\nto or allowed to remain on the site.\n1. THE PARTIES HAVE AGREED THAT\nThe CLO will be employed by the CONTRACTOR on a temporary basis for the duration of the\ncontract to the date of practical completion as defined in the Contract, subject to all the conditions\nset out below.\n1. THE DUTIES OF THE CLO SHALL BE:\n(a) to keep the community informed on the progress of the project;\n(b) to keep the Contractor informed on relevant Community affairs and possible grievances;\n(c) to manage the recruitment of workers from the Sub-Council Job-Seekers Database;\n(d) to assist the Contractor's supervisory staff in the management of the workers.\n1. THE FOLLOWING CONDITIONS OF EMPLOYMENT SHALL APPLY:\nThe Conditions of Temporary Employment as applicable on this Contract for the workers recruited\nfrom the Community shall apply equally to the CLO, except that the rate of remuneration shall be a\nnot less than R200.00 per working day or as stated in the Bill of Quantities. All costs pertaining to the\nCLO must be included in the tendered rates for contractual requirements in Preliminary & General in\nthe Bill of Quantities.These conditions that apply are listed below as they appear in the Contract of\nTemporary Employment:\n3.1 If required to work on a statutory public holiday or Sunday the payment will be double the\namount stated in the previous paragraph.\n3.2 Maximum hours of work:\n(a) 9¼ hours per day\n(b) 45 hours per week;\n(c) 5 days per week;\n(d) 5 hours without an interval, whereupon there shall be an interval of at least minutes;\n(e) A spread-over period of 12 hours.\nPart C 1: Agreement and Contract Data C 1 - 31 C 1.5\nTender Number: NC/15/2026 Community Liaison Officer: 15/2026\n\n3.3 The CLO shall be entitled to payment where the CLO is prevented from working by\nreasons which are within the control of the Contractor.\n3.4 On days when it is raining the Contractor may, before 9 a.m., decide not to open the site\nand there will be no pay.\nIf the Contractor closes the site between 9 a.m. and 1 p.m., the CLO will be paid half the daily\nwage.\nIf the site works later than 1 p.m., the CLO will be paid the full daily wage.\n3.5 Workers and the CLO will not be permitted to work under conditions of:\n(a) abuse of intoxicating substances;\n(b) criminal actions by the employee;\n(c) strike action or political stayaways.\n3.6 Workers, including the CLO, may be dismissed after 2 official written warnings for the\nfollowing behavior:\n(a) undisciplined or unruly behavior;\n(b) insubordination to Team Leader, Supervisors or Management;\n(c) abuse of intoxicating substances;\n(d) willful or negligent damage to or loss of machines or equipment.\nThe Contractor shall ensure that he has statements from at least 2 witnesses’ concerning\nany of the above situations.\nThe Contractor shall inform the CLO within 24 hours of any warning issued to workers\nemployed from the Job-Seekers Database.\n3.7 The CLO will be paid on a Friday afternoon every 4 weeks, 1 week in arrears.\n3.8 The CLO shall be given a statement with each payment on which is recorded:\n(i) the name of the Contractor;\n(ii) the CLO's name;\n(iii) the number of days worked by the CLO;\n(iv) the rate per day;\n(v) the details of any deductions made;\n(vi) the actual amount paid to the CLO.\n3.9 No deduction shall be made from the remuneration except where the CLO consents in\nwriting or unless the Contractor is permitted or required to do so by law or the order of any\ncompetent court.\n3.10 The CLO shall be supplied free of charge with all health and safety equipment required\nby the Occupation Health and Safety Act. The equipment shall remain the property of the\nContractor.\n3.11 The Contractor must give the CLO at least 1 weeks’ notice of the termination of the\nContract of Temporary Employment. If this is not done, the CLO must be paid earnings\nfor 5 days. This condition does not apply if the CLO is dismissed.\n3.12 At the end of the period of temporary employment, the Contractor shall provide a\nCertificate of Service recording the Contractor's name, the CLO's name and address, the\nperiod of service, the type of work on which the CLO was engaged and the rate of\nremuneration on termination.\nPart C 1: Agreement and Contract Data C 1 - 32 C 1.5\nTender Number: NC/15/2026 Community Liaison Officer: 15/2026\n\n1.
